Chal.SnakyCertificate.certificate_correct : Chal.SnakyCertificate.TableValid ∧
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.placementCount = 1837 ∧
    Chal.SnakyCertificate.Output 557 (-1, 0) 118 19 {p | p.1 ≤ -3 ∧ p.2 ≤ -2} ∧
      Chal.SnakyCertificate.Output 595 (-1, 0) 222 27 {p | p.1 = -2 ∧ p.2 ≤ -2} ∧
        Chal.SnakyCertificate.Output 603 (-1, 0) 275 27 {p | p.1 ≤ -3 ∧ p.2 = 0} ∧
          Chal.SnakyCertificate.Output 615 (1, 0) 686 34 {(1, -1)} ∧ Chal.SnakyCertificate.AllRows
@[reducible] def Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell : Type :=
ℤ × ℤ
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.origin : Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
(0, 0)
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.snaky : Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
{(0, 0), (1, 0), (2, 0), (3, 0), (3, 1), (4, 1)}
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.orient : Fin 8 → Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell → Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun r p =>
  match ↑r with
  | 0 => (p.1, p.2)
  | 1 => (p.2, p.1)
  | 2 => (p.1, -p.2)
  | 3 => (-p.2, p.1)
  | 4 => (-p.1, p.2)
  | 5 => (p.2, -p.1)
  | 6 => (-p.1, -p.2)
  | x => (-p.2, -p.1)
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.placement : Fin 8 →
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell → Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell → Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun r t p => Chal.SnakyCertificate.orient r p + t
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.basePoint : Fin 6 → Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun i => ![(0, 0), (1, 0), (2, 0), (3, 0), (3, 1), (4, 1)] i
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.baseEnvelope : Fin 6 → Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun i =>
  Finset.image (Chal.SnakyCertificate.placement 0 (-Chal.SnakyCertificate.basePoint i)) Chal.SnakyCertificate.snaky
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.baseRequired : Fin 6 → Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun i => (Chal.SnakyCertificate.baseEnvelope i).erase Chal.SnakyCertificate.origin

number of parameters: 0
fields:
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term.child : ℕ
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term.rotation : Fin 8
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term.xDigit : Fin 10
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term.yDigit : Fin 10

  Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term.mk (child : ℕ) (rotation : Fin 8) (xDigit yDigit : Fin 10) : Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term.shift : Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term → Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun t => (↑↑t.xDigit - 4, ↑↑t.yDigit - 4)

def Chal.SnakyCertificate.entryAt : ℕ → Chal.SnakyCertificate.Entry :=
fun i => Chal.SnakyCertificate.table[i - 6]?.getD Chal.SnakyCertificate.emptyEntry
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.TableValid : Prop :=
Chal.SnakyCertificate.table.size = 610 ∧
  ∀ k < Chal.SnakyCertificate.table.size,
    have e := Chal.SnakyCertificate.table[k]?.getD Chal.SnakyCertificate.emptyEntry;
    e.index = k + 6 ∧ e.terms ≠ [] ∧ ∀ t ∈ e.terms, t.child < e.index
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.placementCount : ℕ :=
(List.map (fun e => e.terms.length) Chal.SnakyCertificate.table.toList).sum
structure Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData : Type
number of parameters: 0
fields:
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData.A : Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData.H : Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData.height : ℕ
constructor:
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData.mk (A H : Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell) (height : ℕ) :
    Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.emptyRow : Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData :=
{ A := ∅, H := ∅, height := 0 }
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.baseRow : Fin 6 → Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData :=
fun i => { A := Chal.SnakyCertificate.baseRequired i, H := Chal.SnakyCertificate.baseEnvelope i, height := 1 }
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.placeRow : Chal.SnakyCertificate.Term →
  Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData → Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData :=
fun t d =>
  { A := Finset.image (Chal.SnakyCertificate.placement t.rotation t.shift) d.A,
    H := Finset.image (Chal.SnakyCertificate.placement t.rotation t.shift) d.H, height := d.height }
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.unionA : List Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData → Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun ds => List.foldr (fun x1 x2 => x1 ∪ x2) ∅ (List.map Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData.A ds)
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.unionH : List Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData → Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun ds => List.foldr (fun x1 x2 => x1 ∪ x2) ∅ (List.map Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData.H ds)
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.commonH : List Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData → Finset Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell :=
fun ds =>
  match ds with
  | [] => ∅
  | d :: rest => List.foldr (fun x1 x2 => x1 ∩ x2) d.H (List.map Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData.H rest)
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.forkRow : List Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData → Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData :=
fun ds =>
  { A := (Chal.SnakyCertificate.unionA ds ∪ Chal.SnakyCertificate.commonH ds).erase Chal.SnakyCertificate.origin,
    H := insert Chal.SnakyCertificate.origin (Chal.SnakyCertificate.unionH ds),
    height := 1 + List.foldr max 0 (List.map Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData.height ds) }
@[irreducible] def Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt : ℕ → Chal.SnakyCertificate.RowData :=
WellFounded.Nat.fix (fun x => x) fun i a =>
  if h : i < 6 then Chal.SnakyCertificate.baseRow ⟨i, h⟩
  else
    Chal.SnakyCertificate.forkRow
      (List.map
        (fun t =>
          Chal.SnakyCertificate.placeRow t (if x : t.child < i then a t.child ⋯ else Chal.SnakyCertificate.emptyRow))
        (Chal.SnakyCertificate.entryAt i).terms)
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.Output : ℕ → Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell → ℕ → ℕ → Set Chal.SnakyCertificate.Cell → Prop :=
fun i p size height omitted =>
  (Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).A = {p} ∧
    (Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).H.card = size ∧
      (Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).height = height ∧ Disjoint (↑(Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).H) omitted
def Chal.SnakyCertificate.AllRows : Prop :=
∀ i < 616,
  (Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).A ⊆ (Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).H ∧
    Chal.SnakyCertificate.origin ∈ (Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).H ∧
      Chal.SnakyCertificate.origin ∉ (Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).A ∧ (Chal.SnakyCertificate.rowAt i).height ≤ 34
